def smooth_score_array(a, beta):
    # ensure nonnegative finite, maybe scale to sum=1? 
    a = np.maximum(a,0); S=a.sum(); if S<... return inf
    # normalize? to avoid large/small
    p=a/S
    b=fftconv or np.convolve(p,p)
    # smooth max
    z=beta*b
    z=z-zmax; T=np.exp(z).sum(); T=(T*exp(zmax)+?)/beta? Actually smooth max = zmax + log(sum exp(z-zmax))/beta.
    return 2*n*T # since sum=1
```

```
def eval_raw(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); S=a.sum(); if S<... return inf
   b=np.convolve(a,a)  # direct maybe
   M=b.max()
   return 2*n*M/(S*S)
```
Exact np.convolve returns direct. For speed, use FFT for b max if many evals:
```
def convolve_fft(a):
   L=len(a); N=1<<(2*L-1).bit_length()
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   fa*=fa
   b=np.fft.irfft(fa,N)[:2*L-1]
   return b
```
FFT length for n=6733 -> N=16384? 2*6733-1=13465, next power 16384. rfft size 8193, fast. Use this for many evaluations. The FFT may produce tiny negative due roundoff, max unaffected if positive. To be safe, b=np.maximum(b,0) perhaps but direct evaluator doesn't. Since objective exact at final. For optimizer, b min>0; use max(b.real). FFT O(N log N) ~16k*14=236k ops; maybe 0.002s.
```
def conv_max(a): fa=rfft(a,N); return irfft(fa*fa).max()
```

    for iteration in range(max_evals?)
       improved=False

          step=steps[idx]

Convex subproblem direction via penalty:
Given current a (normalized sum=1, M=max b, score=2nM). We want find g≥0 with sum maybe >1 and a*g ≤ M? Since current a*a ≤M. Use multiplicative updates for g from 1 (or normalized). Solve maximize sum g s.t. (a*g)_k ≤ M (elementwise), g≥0.
This is a large linear program with convolution constraints. We can solve/approx with projected gradient on dual or primal penalty.
Primal penalty: maximize G(g) = sum(g) - rho * sum(max(0, (a*g)_k - M)). Since a,g nonnegative, constraints. We can do subgradient ascent:
- start g = a (feasible, sum1). violations v = max(0, conv(a,g)-M). For uniform baseline, g stays.
- subgradient ascent: gradient = 1 - rho * (2 correlation(a, v)) as earlier. Update g ← g + η(gradient), project nonneg, maybe normalize? Since homogeneous? This is coordinate-free and O(n log n) per iteration. Choose rho ~? The constraint values a*g scale ~? a normalized, g sum1, conv ~1/n~1e-4, M~1e-4. rho ~ n maybe. eta ~? We can tune. After some iterations, g may have sum? Unconstrained maximizing sum with penalty; if penalty large, feasible. We can monitor sum and max violation. At end, rescale g to sum1. Evaluate. Accept if better. This may provide a direction that reduces score, especially if f nonuniform.
Let's derive gradient: Loss minimize penalty P = sum max(0, c_k-M), c=a*g. We maximize sum - rho P, so update ascent: g += η [1 - 2 rho corr_positive(a, active)] where active v_k = 1 if c_k>M (subgrad of max can be any in [0,1] if equal; use indicator). For smooth use v_k = (c_k-M)_+ / (M*small) maybe. For large rho, active sparse.
Can implement with FFT to compute c = linear_conv(a,g); then for update grad = 1 - 2 rho * c_active where c_active_m = Σ_{i} active_{m+i} a_i (using correlate). We can compute active correlation via FFT length maybe 3n? Or use np.correlate direct? active length 2n-1, a length n; direct convolution O(n^2)=45M per iterate; maybe okay for few. Use FFT length for product: corr = np.fft.irfft(fft(a)*conj(fft(active?)))? Need orientation. Could use `scipy.signal.fftconvolve(a, active[::-1])` but scipy may be slow overhead. We can implement: We need c_m = Σ_i a_i active_{m+i}. As before full conv with reversed active length n+L-1 ~3n. FFT length P2 = next pow >=3n-2 (32768 for n=6733? 20198 ->32768). One rfft each. Parameter shape:
```
def setup_params(n):
   idx=np.arange(1,n+1,float)
   nidx=np.arange(n,0,-1,float)
   x=(np.arange(n)-0.5)/(n-1)
   logi=np.log(idx); logn=np.log(nidx)
   # basis
   def gauss(mu,s): g=np.exp(-(x-mu)**2/(2*s*s)); return g-g.mean()
   V=[]
   V.append(gauss(0.5,0.15))
   V.append(gauss(0.65,0.10))
   V.append(gauss(0.35,0.10))
   s=np.sin(np.pi*x); V.append(s-s.mean())
   V.append(x-0.5)
   t=(x-0.5)**2; V.append(t-t.mean())
   # normalize each to maxabs maybe 1
   for k in range(len(V)):
      m=np.max(np.abs(V[k])); if m>0: V[k]/=m
   # parameter bounds:
   low=[0.35, -0.4] + [-1.5]*len(V)
   high=[0.65, 0.4] + [1.5]*len(V)
   return ...
```

p=z[0]; q=z[1]
la = -p*data.logi - q*data.logn
for c,V in zip(z[2:], data.V):
    if c != 0: la += c*V
la = la - la.mean()
# clip to [-25,25]
la = np.clip(la, -25, 25)
a = np.exp(la)
return a
```

   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   S=a.sum()
   if S < 1e-12: return inf

   b=np.fft.irfft(f,Nfft)[:2*n-1]

Implement L-BFGS carefully:
```
def lbfags_refine(arr, deadline):
   # arr normalized or raw; convert to y = log(arr+eps) - mean
   a_best = arr/arr.sum()
   y = np.log(np.maximum(a_best, 1e-12))
   y -= y.mean()
   beta = 1e6
   cache = {}
   def fun(y):
       ay = safe_arr_from_y(y)
       # compute a, b, w, T, F, store for grad
       S=ay.sum(); p=ay/S
       f=rfft(p,Nfft); b=irfft(f*f).max? Need full b for weights.
       b = np.fft.irfft(f*f,Nfft)[:2*n-1]
       maxb=b.max(); if not finite return inf
       # weights
       if beta==inf: active... but beta finite
       zeta = (b - maxb)*beta
       # max zeta 0
       expz = np.exp(zeta); Z=expz.sum(); w=expz/Z; T=maxb + np.log(Z)/beta
       F=2*n*T
       cache['a']=ay; cache['b']=b; cache['w']=w; cache['T']=T; cache['maxb']=maxb; cache['last_y']=y.copy()? maybe not.
       return F
   def fprime(y):
       # ensure cache from fun
       a=cache['a']; w=cache['w']; T=cache['T']
       # if y differs from cached? We can not compare; use cache as current if same call. But L-BFGS may call fprime after fun with same y.
       z=corr_from_weights(a,w)
       S=a.sum()
       grad_a = (4*n/S/S)*z - (4*n*T/(S**3))*np.ones(n)
       gy = a*grad_a
       gy -= gy.mean()
       return gy
   res=opt.minimize(fun, y, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter': ...,'maxfun': ...,'ftol':1e-8,'gtol':1e-6, 'maxls':30})
```

Correlation function for L-BFGS:
```
def eval_corr(a, w, Ncorr, Lw):
    wr = w[::-1]
    Fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Ncorr)
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(wr, Ncorr)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fw, Ncorr)
    idx = Lw - 1 - np.arange(n)
    return corr[idx]
```
This creates `wr` copy length Lw ~13465 each grad; okay. Could precompute indices. `idx` precomputed. `corr[idx]` returns copy.

def fun(y):
    ny = y - y.mean()
    ny = np.clip(ny, -15,15)
    a = np.exp(ny)
    S = a.sum()
    if S <=0: return 1e100
    p = a/S
    f = np.fft.rfft(p, Nfft)
    f *= f
    b = np.fft.irfft(f, Nfft)[:Lb]
    maxb = float(np.max(b))
    maxb_norm = maxb / (S*S) if S>0 else 0.0? Wait p sum1 already, b of p. maxb_norm = maxb (since p). Correct no divide. p sum1.
    beta = 500.0 / maxb_norm  # since p
    zs = (b - maxb)*beta
    expz = np.exp(np.clip(zs, -745,0.0))
    Z = expz.sum()
    if not finite Z or Z<=0: w = np.zeros(Lb); w[argmax]=1; T=maxb
    else: w=expz/Z; T=maxb + math.log(Z)/beta
    F = 2.0*n*T
    cache['a']=a; cache['p']=p; cache['w']=w; cache['T']=T
    return F
```

Let's think about matrix-free minimization of F(a)=2n M/S² with a≥0. Since scale-invariant, fix S=1. We can use FISTA for smoothed max with projection onto simplex (a≥0, sum1). This is a convex? F_smooth not convex maybe, but first-order can move. Projected gradient: at each step compute p (sum1), weights, gradient of T (wrt p): 2 z_p. Gradient of objective on simplex (sum fixed) is grad =2z_p + λ? Since sum constraint, use grad - mean(grad). Update p ← project(p - η grad). Nonneg and sum1. This is similar to L-BFGS but coordinate-free and can run many small steps. It may be easier to tune and time-controlled. It requires corr of p and w each step (Ncorr). We can run maybe 1000 steps. Step size? Objective values ~1.5, gradient scale? M~1e-4, grad_m=2z_p where z_p~1/n? ~1e-4, so η~100 maybe. We can backtracking.
Algorithm:
```
p = best_arr/sum; p = project simplex
for steps:
  b=conv(p,p); max; w; T=smooth; grad=2*corr(p,w)
  grad -= grad.mean()
  # line search or fixed eta
  for trial eta in [eta, eta*0.5,...]: q=project_simplex(p - eta*grad); if F(q)<F(p): p=q; eta*=1.5; break else eta*=0.5
```
Projection to simplex: sort algorithm O(n log n) per trial ~6700 log, maybe 0.1ms? In Python sorting 6733 ~0.5ms. 1000*line search maybe 1s? Actually 6733 sort in numpy ~1ms; 10000 10s. Could be okay.
But F not convex; projected gradient may oscillate. Use averaged iterate? We can use weights w uniform over max plateau for exact subgradient. This is more robust than L-BFGS? Maybe.

    def make_arr(z):
       la = -z[0]*logi - z[1]*logn
       for k in range(D):
          c=z[2+k]
          if c != 0:
             la = la + c*V[k]
       la = la - la.mean()

       return np.exp(la)

    def score_arr(a):
       a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
       if a.size!=n: return 1e100
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return 1e100
       S=a.sum()
       if S < 1e-12: return 1e100
       p=a/S
       f=np.fft.rfft(p,Nfft)
       f=f*f
       b=np.fft.irfft(f,Nfft)[:Lb]
       m=float(np.max(b))
       if not np.isfinite(m) or m<0: m=0.0
       return float(2.0*n*m)
```

Cache as dict.
```
    def lbfags_refine(deadline_l):
       nonlocal best_score,best_arr
       cache={'a':None,'S':1.0,'T':1e-9,'w':None}
       refine_best_score = best_score; refine_best_a = best_arr.copy()
       idx_const? 
       def corr(a,w): ...
       def fun(y):
          ny=y-y.mean(); ny=np.clip(ny,-15,15); a=np.exp(ny); S=a.sum()
          if not finite or S<1e-8: return 1e100
          p=a/S
          f=np.fft.rfft(p,Nfft); b=np.fft.irfft(f*f,Nfft)[:Lb]
          maxb=float(np.max(b)); if maxb<=0: T=0; w all? F=0? impossible.
          beta = 800.0/maxb if maxb>0 else 1e6
          zs=(b-maxb)*beta
          expz=np.exp(np.clip(zs,-745,0.0)); Z=expz.sum()
          if Z>0: w=expz/Z; T=maxb + math.log(Z)/beta
          else: w=np.zeros(Lb); w[int(np.argmax(b))]=1.0; T=maxb
          F=2*n*T
          cache['a']=a; cache['S']=S; cache['T']=T; cache['w']=w
          if F < refine_best_score:
              refine_best_score=F; refine_best_a=a.copy()
          return F
       def fprime(y):
          a=cache['a']; w=cache['w']; S=cache['S']; T=cache['T']
          if a is None: return np.zeros(n)
          # corr
          wr=w[::-1]
          Fa=np.fft.rfft(a,Ncorr); Fw=np.fft.rfft(wr,Ncorr)
          corr=np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fw,Ncorr)
          z=corr[expr_indices]  # expr_indices = Lb-1 - arange(n)
          grad_a = (4.0*n/(S*S))*z - (4.0*n*T/S)*np.ones(n)
          gy = a*grad_a
          gy -= gy.mean()
          # if nonfinite return zeros
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(gy)): return np.zeros(n)
          return gy
       try:
          res=_scipy_minimize(fun, y0, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter': 200, 'maxfun': maxfun, 'maxls':30, 'ftol':1e-9, 'gtol':1e-6, 'disp':False})
          # update with refine_best_a or res.x
       except: pass
       if refine_best_score < best_score:
          best_score=refine_best_score; best_arr=refine_best_a.copy()
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None


def _next_pow2(m: int) -> int:
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    hard_deadline = t_start + max(0.0, budget - 5.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Parent construction, if present.
    parent = None
    try:
        parr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if parr.ndim == 1 and parr.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(parr)):
            parr = np.clip(parr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(np.sum(parr)) >= 0.01:
                parent = parr
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        n = int(parent.size)
    else:
        n = 6733

    if n < 2:
        return [0.5, 0.5]

    # Do not let unexpectedly large parents consume the sandbox.
    if n > 12000:
        n = 12000
        parent = None

    Lb = 2 * n - 1
    Nfft = _next_pow2(Lb)
    Ncorr = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    nidx = np.arange(n, 0, -1, dtype=np.float64)
    logi = np.log(idx)
    logn = np.log(nidx)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)

    def score_array(a):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.ndim != 1 or a.size != n:
            return float("inf")
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            return float("inf")
        a = np.where(a < 0.0, 0.0, a)
        a = np.where(a > 1000.0, 1000.0, a)
        S = float(np.sum(a))
        if S < 1e-12:
            return float("inf")
        p = a / S
        fa = np.fft.rfft(p, Nfft)
        spec = fa * fa.conj()
        b = np.fft.irfft(spec, Nfft)[:Lb]
        m = float(np.max(b))
        if not np.isfinite(m):
            return float("inf")
        return float(2.0 * n * m)

    best_score = float("inf")
    best_arr = None
    pool = []

    def consider(a, pool_margin=0.35):
        nonlocal best_score, best_arr
        try:
            a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
            if a.ndim != 1 or a.size != n:
                return
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
                return
            a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(np.sum(a)) < 1e-12:
                return
            s = score_array(a)
            if not np.isfinite(s):
                return
            if s < best_score:
                best_score = s
                best_arr = a.copy()
            if len(pool) < 80 or s < best_score + pool_margin:
                pool.append((s, a.copy()))
                if len(pool) > 80:
                    pool.pop(0)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Basic inverse-square-root seed.
    inv_sqrt = 1.0 / np.sqrt(idx)
    consider(inv_sqrt)

    if parent is not None:
        consider(parent)

    # Low-dimensional shape family:
    # a_i proportional to (i+1)^(-p) * (n-i)^(-q) * exp(sum c_k v_k(x_i)).
    D = 6
    V = []

    def _gauss(mu, sig):
        g = np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - mu) / sig) ** 2)
        g -= g.mean()
        mx = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if mx > 0:
            g /= mx
        return g

    V.append(_gauss(0.50, 0.15))
    V.append(_gauss(0.65, 0.10))
    V.append(_gauss(0.35, 0.10))

    s1 = np.sin(np.pi * x)
    s1 -= s1.mean()
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(s1)))
    if mx > 0:
        s1 /= mx
    V.append(s1)

    lin = x - 0.5
    lin -= lin.mean()
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(lin)))
    if mx > 0:
        lin /= mx
    V.append(lin)

    quad = (x - 0.5) ** 2
    quad -= quad.mean()
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(quad)))
    if mx > 0:
        quad /= mx
    V.append(quad)

    low = np.array([0.35, -0.4] + [-1.5] * D, dtype=np.float64)
    high = np.array([0.65, 0.4] + [1.5] * D, dtype=np.float64)

    def make_arr(z):
        z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
        if z.size != 2 + D:
            return None
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
            return None
        if np.any(z < low) or np.any(z > high):
            return None
        la = -z[0] * logi - z[1] * logn
        for k in range(D):
            c = z[2 + k]
            if abs(c) > 1e-12:
                la = la + c * V[k]
        la = la - la.mean()
        la = np.clip(la, -15.0, 15.0)
        return np.exp(la)

    z_base = np.array([0.5, 0.0] + [0.0] * D, dtype=np.float64)
    consider(make_arr(z_base))

    # Helper explicit grid around plausible shapes.
    plain_gauss = {}
    for mu, sig in [(0.50, 0.15), (0.65, 0.10), (0.35, 0.10), (0.50, 0.30)]:
        plain_gauss[(mu, sig)] = np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - mu) / sig) ** 2)

    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 80.0:
        for p in (0.46, 0.48, 0.50, 0.52, 0.54):
            for q in (-0.15, 0.0, 0.15):
                la = -p * logi - q * logn
                consider(np.exp(la - la.mean()))

        for c in (0.15, 0.35, 0.6, 1.0):
            la = -0.5 * logi + c * V[0]
            consider(np.exp(la - la.mean()))

        for c in (0.2, 0.5, 0.8):
            la = -0.5 * logi + c * np.sin(np.pi * x)
            consider(np.exp(la - la.mean()))
            la2 = -0.5 * logi - c * np.sin(np.pi * x)
            consider(np.exp(la2 - la2.mean()))

        for c in (0.15, -0.15, 0.35, -0.35):
            la = -0.5 * logi + c * (x - 0.5)
            consider(np.exp(la - la.mean()))

        for key, g in plain_gauss.items():
            for c in (0.3, 0.7, 1.2):
                la = -0.5 * logi + c * g
                consider(np.exp(la - la.mean()))

    # Profile one evaluation for budgeting.
    def param_eval(z):
        a = make_arr(z)
        if a is None:
            return float("inf"), None
        s = score_array(a)
        return s, a

    if best_arr is None:
        best_arr = inv_sqrt
        best_score = score_array(inv_sqrt)

    t_prof = time.monotonic()
    for _ in range(6):
        param_eval(z_base)
    t_eval = max(1e-5, (time.monotonic() - t_prof) / 6.0)

    # Coordinate-free derivative-free refinement of low-dimensional parameters.
    nm_deadline = hard_deadline - 260.0
    if _scipy_minimize is not None and time.monotonic() < nm_deadline:
        starts = [z_base.copy()]
        z1 = z_base.copy(); z1[2] = 0.7
        starts.append(z1)
        z2 = z_base.copy(); z2[2] = 0.5; z2[3] = 0.45
        starts.append(z2)
        z3 = z_base.copy(); z3[2] = 0.55; z3[3] = 0.65; z3[4] = 0.25
        starts.append(z3)
        z4 = z_base.copy(); z4[0] = 0.48; z4[2] = 0.35
        starts.append(z4)
        z5 = z_base.copy(); z5[0] = 0.52; z5[2] = 0.35
        starts.append(z5)

        for _ in range(8):
            z = z_base.copy()
            z[0] += rng.normal(0.0, 0.02)
            z[1] += rng.normal(0.0, 0.04)
            z[2:] += rng.normal(0.0, 0.22, D)
            starts.append(np.clip(z, low, high))

        rem = max(0.0, nm_deadline - time.monotonic())
        total_fev = int(max(1200, min(9000, rem / max(t_eval * 1.4, 1e-4))))

        def run_nm(z0, fev):
            nonlocal best_score, best_arr
            if fev < 40:
                return
            z0 = np.asarray(z0, dtype=np.float64)
            best_local_f = float("inf")
            best_local_a = None

            def fun(z):
                nonlocal best_local_f, best_local_a
                z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
                s, a = param_eval(z)
                if not np.isfinite(s):
                    return 1e50
                if s < best_local_f:
                    best_local_f = s
                    best_local_a = a.copy()
                if s < best_score:
                    best_score = s
                    best_arr = a.copy()
                    pool.append((s, a.copy()))
                    if len(pool) > 80:
                        pool.pop(0)
                return s

            simplex = [z0.copy()]
            steps = np.array([0.02, 0.05] + [0.30] * D, dtype=np.float64)
            for i in range(len(z0)):
                zz = z0.copy()
                zz[i] += steps[i]
                simplex.append(zz)

            options = {"maxfev": int(fev), "xatol": 1e-5, "fatol": 1e-7}
            try:
                res = _scipy_minimize(
                    fun, z0, method="Nelder-Mead",
                    initial_simplex=simplex, options=options
                )
                s, a = param_eval(res.x)
                if np.isfinite(s):
                    if s < best_local_f:
                        best_local_f = s
                        best_local_a = a
                    if s < best_score:
                        best_score = s
                        best_arr = a.copy()
                if best_local_a is not None and best_local_f < best_score:
                    best_score = best_local_f
                    best_arr = best_local_a.copy()
            except Exception:
                if best_local_a is not None and best_local_f < best_score:
                    best_score = best_local_f
                    best_arr = best_local_a.copy()

        for i, z0 in enumerate(starts):
            if time.monotonic() > nm_deadline - 30.0:
                break
            if i == 0:
                fev = int(total_fev * 0.34)
            elif i < 4:
                fev = int(total_fev * 0.13)
            else:
                fev = int(total_fev * 0.05)
            fev = max(120, min(fev, int(max(50, (nm_deadline - time.monotonic()) / max(t_eval * 1.4, 1e-4)))))
            if fev < 60:
                break
            run_nm(z0, fev)

    # Full-dimensional log-space L-BFGS polishing from the best shape found so far.
    expr_idx = np.arange(Lb - 1, Lb - 1 - n, -1, dtype=np.intp)

    if _scipy_minimize is not None and best_arr is not None and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 160.0:
        a0 = np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=np.float64)
        a0 = np.clip(a0, 0.0, 1000.0)
        S0 = float(np.sum(a0))
        if S0 > 1e-12:
            p0 = a0 / S0
            p0 = np.maximum(p0, 1e-12)
            y0 = np.log(p0)
            y0 -= y0.mean()

            lbf_deadline = hard_deadline - 70.0
            rem = max(0.0, lbf_deadline - time.monotonic())
            maxfun = int(min(4500, max(700, rem / max(t_eval * 5.0, 1e-4))))

            cache = {"a": None, "S": 1.0, "T": 0.0, "w": None}
            refine_best_score = float(best_score)
            refine_best_a = np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=np.float64).copy()

            def lbf_fun(y):
                nonlocal refine_best_score, refine_best_a
                ny = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64) - float(np.mean(y))
                ny = np.clip(ny, -15.0, 15.0)
                a = np.exp(ny)
                S = float(np.sum(a))
                if not np.isfinite(S) or S < 1e-8:
                    return 1e50
                p = a / S
                fa = np.fft.rfft(p, Nfft)
                spec = fa * fa.conj()
                b = np.fft.irfft(spec, Nfft)[:Lb]
                maxb = float(np.max(b))
                if not np.isfinite(maxb) or maxb <= 0.0:
                    return 1e50
                beta = 800.0 / maxb
                zs = (b - maxb) * beta
                zs = np.clip(zs, -745.0, 0.0)
                ez = np.exp(zs)
                Z = float(np.sum(ez))
                if Z > 0.0 and np.isfinite(Z):
                    w = ez / Z
                    T = maxb + math.log(Z) / beta
                else:
                    w = np.zeros(Lb, dtype=np.float64)
                    w[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
                    T = maxb
                F = 2.0 * n * T
                cache["a"] = a
                cache["S"] = S
                cache["T"] = float(T)
                cache["w"] = w
                if F < refine_best_score:
                    refine_best_score = F
                    refine_best_a = a.copy()
                return F

            def lbf_prime(y):
                a = cache.get("a")
                w = cache.get("w")
                S = cache.get("S", 1.0)
                T = cache.get("T", 0.0)
                if a is None or w is None:
                    return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                wr = w[::-1]
                Fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Ncorr)
                Fw = np.fft.rfft(wr, Ncorr)
                corr = np.fft.irfft(Fa * Fw, Ncorr)
                z = corr[expr_idx]
                grad_a = (4.0 * n / (S * S)) * z - (4.0 * n * T / S) * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
                gy = a * grad_a
                gy -= gy.mean()
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(gy)):
                    return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                return gy

            try:
                res = _scipy_minimize(
                    lbf_fun, y0, method="L-BFGS-B",
                    options={
                        "maxiter": 220,
                        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                        "maxls": 30,
                        "ftol": 1e-9,
                        "gtol": 1e-6,
                        "disp": False,
                    }
                )
                if refine_best_score < best_score:
                    best_score = refine_best_score
                    best_arr = refine_best_a.copy()
                    pool.append((best_score, best_arr.copy()))
            except Exception:
                if refine_best_score < best_score:
                    best_score = refine_best_score
                    best_arr = refine_best_a.copy()
                    pool.append((best_score, best_arr.copy()))

    # Small array-level block multiplicative local search.
    if best_arr is not None and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 50.0:
        a_loc = np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        S_loc = float(np.sum(a_loc))
        if S_loc > 1e-12:
            a_loc /= S_loc
        local_best_score = score_array(a_loc)
        if local_best_score < best_score:
            best_score = local_best_score
            best_arr = a_loc.copy()

        local_deadline = hard_deadline - 40.0
        while time.monotonic() < local_deadline:
            xa = np.asarray(a_loc, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            width = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 80), max(2, n // 8)))
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            delta = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.28))
            if abs(delta) < 1e-6:
                continue
            xa[start:start + width] *= math.exp(delta)
            s = score_array(xa)
            if np.isfinite(s) and s < local_best_score - 1e-9:
                local_best_score = s
                a_loc = xa
                if s < best_score:
                    best_score = s
                    best_arr = xa.copy()
                    pool.append((s, xa.copy()))
        if local_best_score < best_score:
            best_score = local_best_score
            best_arr = a_loc.copy()
            pool.append((best_score, a_loc.copy()))

    # Exact final validation of the strongest internal candidates.
    if best_arr is None:
        best_arr = inv_sqrt
    pool.append((float(score_array(best_arr)), np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
    if parent is not None:
        pool.append((float(score_array(parent)), np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
    pool.append((float(score_array(inv_sqrt)), np.asarray(inv_sqrt, dtype=np.float64)))

    try:
        pool.sort(key=lambda t: t[0])
    except Exception:
        pool = []

    exact_best_score = float("inf")
    exact_best_arr = None

    def exact_consider(a):
        nonlocal exact_best_score, exact_best_arr
        try:
            a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
            if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
                return
            a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
            m = float(np.max(a))
            if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
                return
            out = (a / m) * 0.5
            out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(np.sum(out)) < 0.01:
                return
            lst = [float(v) for v in out]
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < exact_best_score:
                exact_best_score = sc
                exact_best_arr = out.copy()
        except Exception:
            return

    checked = 0
    for _, a in pool:
        exact_consider(a)
        checked += 1
        if checked >= 14 or time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 8.0:
            break

    if exact_best_arr is None:
        exact_best_arr = np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=np.float64)

    out = np.asarray(exact_best_arr, dtype=np.float64)
    if out.ndim != 1 or out.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(out)):
        out = np.full(64, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)

    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    m = float(np.max(out))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        out = np.full(64, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        out = (out / m) * 0.5

    lst = [float(v) for v in out]
    try:
        sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            lst = [0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5]
    except Exception:
        lst = [0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5]

    return lst
```
